C.5.4
Proof of Lemma
11

Proof of Lemma
11
.

Observe that

|\mathcal{Z}|=\frac{n!}{\big((q+1)!\big)^{r}(q!)^{k-r}}.

By the bounds of
Robbins 1955
, for any
m\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
,

\sqrt{2\pi}m^{m+1/2}\exp\{-m+1/(12m+1)\}<m!<\sqrt{2\pi}m^{m+1/2}\exp\{-m+1/(12m)\}.

Applying these to
n!
,
(q+1)!
and
q!
, we obtain that

\log\big(|\mathcal{Z}|\big)\geq n\log(k)-C_{1}k\log(n),

(114)

for some absolute constant
C_{1}>0
.

Fix any labeling
Z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}
.
For
0\leq s\leq n
, the number of labelings that differ from
Z^{\prime}
in exactly
s
positions is at most

\binom{n}{s}(k-1)^{s},

since we choose the
s
changed positions and assign to each one of the
k-1
other labels.
Hence, the number of labelings within Hamming distance at most
t
of
Z^{\prime}
satisfies

V_{k}(n,t)=\sum_{s=0}^{t}\binom{n}{s}(k-1)^{s}.

Let
0<\delta<1/2
and set
t=\lfloor\delta n\rfloor
.
By
Lemma

12
, we can derive that

\sum_{s=0}^{\lfloor\delta n\rfloor}\binom{n}{s}\leq\exp\big\{nH(\delta)\big\},\quad\mbox{where }H(\delta)=-\delta\log(\delta)-(1-\delta)\log(1-\delta).

(115)

Thus, we have that

\displaystyle V_{k}(n,\lfloor\delta n\rfloor)\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{\lfloor\delta n\rfloor}\binom{n}{s}(k-1)^{\delta n}\leq(k-1)^{\delta n}\exp\big\{nH(\delta)\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\Big\{n\big\{H(\delta)+\delta\log(k-1)\big\}\Big\},

(116)

where the second inequality follows from (
115
).

We now construct
\mathcal{Z}^{*}
via the usual greedy packing procedure

•

Initialize
\mathcal{R}=\mathcal{Z}
and
\mathcal{Z}^{*}=\emptyset
.

•

While
\mathcal{R}\neq\emptyset
: pick any
Z\in\mathcal{R}
, add
Z
to
\mathcal{Z}^{*}
and remove from
\mathcal{R}
all labelings within Hamming distance at most
\lfloor\delta n\rfloor
of
Z
.

Then for any two distinct
Z,Z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}^{*}
, we have
d_{H}(Z,Z^{\prime})>\lfloor\delta n\rfloor
.
Since each step removes at most
V_{k}(n,\lfloor\delta n\rfloor)
labelings, we have that

|\mathcal{Z}^{*}|\geq\frac{|\mathcal{Z}|}{V_{k}(n,\lfloor\delta n\rfloor)}.

(117)

Combining (
114
), (
C.5.4
) and (
117
) yields that

\log\big(|\mathcal{Z}^{*}|\big)\geq n\log(k)-C_{1}k\log(n)-n\big\{H(\delta)+\delta\log(k-1)\big\}.

(118)

Choose
\delta=0.1
, we have
H(0.1)\leq 0.3251
and for any
k\geq 2
,

H(\delta)+\delta\log(k-1)=H(0.1)+0.1\log(k-1)\leq 0.3251+0.1\log(k)\leq 2^{-1}\log(k).

(119)

Combining (
118
) and (
119
), there exists absolute constant
C_{2}>0
such that

\log\big(|\mathcal{Z}^{*}|\big)\geq n\log(k)-C_{1}k\log(n)-2^{-1}n\log(k)=2^{-1}n\log(k)-C_{1}k\log(n)\geq C_{2}n\log(k),

which completes the proof.
∎

C.5.5
Proof of Lemma
12

Proof of Lemma
12
.

Let
X\sim\mathrm{Bin}(n,1/2)
. Then

\mathbb{P}(X=s)=2^{-n}\binom{n}{s}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sum_{s=0}^{\lfloor\delta n\rfloor}\binom{n}{s}=2^{n}\mathbb{P}\{X\leq\lfloor\delta n\rfloor\}\leq 2^{n}\mathbb{P}\{X\leq\delta n\}.

(120)

For any
t<0
, by Markov’s inequality, we have that

\mathbb{P}\{X\leq\delta n\}=\mathbb{P}\Big\{e^{tX}\geq e^{t\delta n}\Big\}\leq e^{-t\delta n}\mathbb{E}\big[\exp\{tX\}\big].

(121)

Since
X=\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}
with
Y_{i}\overset{\mbox{i.i.d.}}{\sim}\mathrm{Bernoulli}(1/2)
, we can derive that

\mathbb{E}\big[\exp\{tX\}\big]=\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\big[\exp\{tY_{i}\}\big]=\Big[\mathbb{E}\big[\exp\{tY_{1}\}\big]\Big]^{n}=\big\{(1+e^{t})/2\big\}^{n}.

(122)

Combining (
121
) and (
122
) yields that

\mathbb{P}\{X\leq\delta n\}\leq\exp\Big[n\big\{-t\delta+\log\big((1+e^{t})/2\big)\big\}\Big].

(123)

Define

\phi(t)=-t\delta+\log\big((1+e^{t})/2\big),\quad\forall t\in\mathbb{R}.

Then, we have that

\phi^{\prime}(t)=-\delta+\frac{e^{t}}{1+e^{t}},\quad\phi^{\prime\prime}(t)=\frac{e^{t}}{(1+e^{t})^{2}}>0,\quad\forall t\in\mathbb{R}.

Hence,
\phi
is convex and its minimizer satisfies
\phi^{\prime}(t)=0
, giving

t=\log\bigg(\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\bigg)<0,

where the last inequality follows from
0<\delta<1/2
.

Substitute this
t
into
\phi(t)
gives

\displaystyle\phi(t)\geq

\displaystyle\phi\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\bigg)\bigg)=-\delta\log\bigg(\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\bigg)+\log\bigg(2^{-1}\bigg(1+\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta\log\delta-(1-\delta)\log(1-\delta)-\log 2=H(\delta)-\log 2.

(124)

Combining (
120
), (
123
) and (
C.5.5
), we finally obtain that

\sum_{s=0}^{\lfloor\delta n\rfloor}\binom{n}{s}\leq 2^{n}\exp\Big\{n\big\{H(\delta)-\log(2)\big\}\Big\}=\exp\big\{nH(\delta)\big\},

which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Technical details for results in Section
3

All auxiliary results for the non-stationary setting are collected in Appendix
D.1
, with their proofs provided in Appendix
D.3
. The proofs of the main results in Section
3
, namely
Theorem

4
and
Proposition

5
, are given in Appendix
D.2
.

D.1
Additional results

We collect several auxiliary results for the non-stationary AR(1)-MSBM defined in
Definition

2
. These results characterize temporal dependence, bias of local estimators and spectral properties of window-averaged tensors, which are key ingredients for the analysis in Section
3
.

We first establish a strong mixing property for edgewise processes, extending
Lemma

7
to the non-stationary setting.

Lemma 13
.

Let the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
be defined in
Definition

2
. For any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
, the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}_{i,j,l}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
is strongly mixing.

We next quantify the temporal dependence of the edgewise processes. The following lemma shows that the covariance between edge variables is non-negative and decays geometrically with the time lag.

Lemma 14
.

Let the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
be defined in
Definition

2
. For any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
and
r,s\in[T]
, it holds that

0\leq\mathrm{Cov}(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r},\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s})\leq\frac{1}{4}(1-2c_{\min})^{|s-r|},

We now analyze the bias of the local look-back estimators for the transition probabilities.

Proposition 15
.

Let the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
be defined in
Definition

2
and let
G
denote the number of
V
-quasi-stationary segments in
\{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
as defined in
Definition

4
. Suppose
2
(i)
-
(iii)
hold.
Then for any
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
, there exists an absolute constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{E}\big\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big\}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\Big|\leq

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big|+\frac{C}{k},\quad\forall 1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L],

(125)

and

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{E}\big\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big\}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\Big|\leq

\displaystyle\big|\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big|+\frac{C}{k},\quad\forall 1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L].

(126)

Here
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t,k}
and
\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t,k}
are defined in (
132
). If
k=1
, then

\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}=\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}=0;

and if
t-k\geq T_{g}+1
for some
g\in[G]
, then

\big|\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big|\leq\frac{1}{2c_{\min}^{2}}\max_{s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-k]}|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\sum_{u^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{t}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big),

and

\big|\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big|\leq\frac{1}{2c_{\min}^{2}}\max_{s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-k]}|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\sum_{u^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{t}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big).

We now establish spectral properties of the window-averaged transition probability tensors in the non-stationary regime. The next lemma shows that their Tucker ranks and singular values behave analogously to the stationary case described in
Lemma

9
.

Lemma 16
.

For any
t\in[T]
and any look-back window size
k\in[t]
, let
\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times K\times L}
be the tensor defined in
2
(iv)
. Assume that
\mathrm{rank}\big(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})\big)=K
and denote
\bar{r}^{t,k}=\mathrm{rank}\big(\mathcal{M}_{3}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})\big)
. Define

\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{t,k}=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{u=t-k+1}^{t}\ (\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}).

Then, for any
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
, we have that

\mathrm{rank}\big(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k})\big)=\mathrm{rank}\big(\mathcal{M}_{2}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k})\big)=K,\quad\mathrm{rank}\big(\mathcal{M}_{3}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k})\big)=\bar{r}^{t,k},

and

s_{\min}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})\leq\sigma_{\min}\big(\mathcal{M}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{t,k})\big)\leq\big\|\mathcal{M}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{t,k})\big\|\leq s_{\max}\|\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}\|,\quad\forall s\in[3],

where
s_{\min}=\min_{j\in[K]}s_{j}
and
s_{\max}=\max_{j\in[K]}s_{j}
with for any
j\in[K]
,
s_{j}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i,j}
.

Finally, we establish an error bound for the subspace estimator obtained via H-PCA (
Stage II
of
Algorithm

2
).

Proposition 17
.

Let the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
be defined in
Definition

2
and let
G
be the number of
V
-quasi-stationary segments in
\{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
as defined in
Definition

4
. Suppose that
2
holds.
Define

\tau(t)=C_{\tau}\max\Big\{t^{-1}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T),\big(V(t)\big)^{2}\Big\},\quad\forall t\in[T],

(127)

where
C_{\tau}>0
is a sufficiently large constant. For any
g\in[G]
and any
t\in[T_{g}]\backslash[T_{g-1}]
, under
Algorithm

2
, with probability at least
1-(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c}
,

\inf_{O\in\mathbb{O}_{K\times K}}\|\widehat{U}^{t}_{Z}-U_{Z}O\|\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t},

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}=C\sigma_{Q}^{-2}\bigg(\frac{K^{2}\sqrt{L}}{n}\epsilon_{t}^{2}+K^{2}L\epsilon_{t}^{4}\bigg)+C\sigma_{Q}^{-1}\bigg(\frac{K}{\sqrt{n}}\epsilon_{t}+K\sqrt{L}\epsilon_{t}^{2}\bigg),

(128)

with

\epsilon_{t}=\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}}},V(t-T_{g-1})\bigg\},\quad\sigma_{Q}=\min_{t\in[T],s\in[t]}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,s}),

(129)

and absolute constants
C,c>0
.

When
K
are treated as a constant and
\sigma_{Q}\asymp\sqrt{L}
, the bound in (
128
) simplifies to

\frac{\epsilon_{t}}{\sqrt{nL}}+\epsilon_{t}^{2}+\epsilon_{t}^{4}.

(130)

Thus, a smaller
\epsilon_{t}
leads to a sharper bound.
However,
\epsilon_{t}
itself reflects a trade-off between the segment length
(t-T_{g-1})
and the drift envelope
V(\cdot)
:
a fast-decaying
V(\cdot)
creates more and shorter segments and increases the stochastic term
(t-T_{g-1})^{-1/2}
, whereas a slow-decaying
V(\cdot)
yields longer segments and reduces stochastic variability, but at the cost of a larger drift term
V(t-T_{g-1})
. For example, if

V(t)=t^{-1/2},\quad\forall t>0,

then up to logarithmic factors,

\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{t}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{(t-T_{g-1})nL}}+\frac{1}{t-T_{g-1}}

which matches the stationary-rate bound in (
12
), when restricted to each quasi-stationary segment.
This shows that
Algorithm

2
automatically adapts its look-back window: it contracts the window in the presence of substantial cumulative drift or abrupt changes, while expanding it to the largest statistically reliable size when the process remains quasi-stationary.

D.2
Proofs for Section
3

To establish the theoretical results for
Algorithm

2
, we first analyze the windowed estimators of the transition probabilities,
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}
and
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}
.
This analysis relies on two key ingredients: (i) a concentration inequality (
Theorem

18
, with proof provided in Appendix
D.2.1
), and (ii) a bias control result (
Proposition

19
, with proof provided in Appendix
D.2.2
).

Theorem 18
.

Let the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
be defined as in
Definition

2
. Let
G
be the number of
V
-quasi-stationary segments in
\{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
as defined in
Definition

4
. Suppose that
2
(i)
holds.
For
Algorithm

2
, define

\tau(t)=C_{\tau}\max\Big\{t^{-1}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T),\big(V(t)\big)^{2}\Big\},\quad\forall t\in[T]

where
C_{\tau}>0
is a sufficiently large constant.
Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\leq C\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big(V(t-T_{g-1})\big)^{2}\bigg\},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\forall 1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L],\mbox{ for any }t\in[T_{g}]/[T_{g-1}]\mbox{ with }g\in[G]\bigg\}\geq 1-(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c},

(131)

for some absolute constants
C,c>0
.

Proposition 19
.

Let the process
\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset\{0,1\}^{n\times n\times L}
be defined in
Definition

2
, and let
G
be the number of
V
-quasi-stationary segments in
\{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
as defined in
Definition

4
. Suppose that
2
holds. For
Algorithm

2
, let

\tau(t)=C_{\tau}\max\Big\{t^{-1}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T),\big(V(t)\big)^{2}\Big\},\quad\forall t\in[T],

where
C_{\tau}>0
is a sufficiently large constant. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\max\Big\{\Big|\mathbb{E}\big\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}\big\}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}\Big|,\Big|\mathbb{E}\big\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}\big\}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}\Big|\Big\}>C\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\big(V(t-T_{g-1})\big)^{2}\bigg\},1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L],\mbox{ for any }t\in[T_{g}]/[T_{g-1}]\mbox{ with }g\in[G]\bigg]\leq(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c},

for some absolute constants
C,c>0
. Here, for any
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
, we define

\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t,k}=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{u=t-k+1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t,k}=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{u=t-k+1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}.

(132)

Based on
Theorem

18
,
Proposition

19
and the spectral properties of the window-averaged transition probability tensors established in
Lemma

16
, the proofs of
Theorem

4
,
Proposition

5
and
Proposition

17

follow the same structure as their stationary counterparts
(
Theorem

1
,
Proposition

3
and
Proposition

10
).
We therefore omit the details.

D.2.1
Proof of Theorem
18

The following notion of functional similarity was introduced by
Huang and Wang 2024
and we copy it here for completeness.

Definition 7

(Closeness)
.

Let
f,g\colon\mathcal{X}\to\mathbb{R}
be lower bounded functions and
\varepsilon,\delta\geq 0
. We say functions
f
and
g
are
(\varepsilon,\delta)
-close, if for all
x\in\mathcal{X}
,

g(x)-\inf_{x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}}g(x^{\prime})\leq e^{\varepsilon}\Big(f(x)-\inf_{x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}}f(x^{\prime})+\delta\Big),

and

f(x)-\inf_{x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}}f(x^{\prime})\leq e^{\varepsilon}\Big(g(x)-\inf_{x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}}g(x^{\prime})+\delta\Big).

We now introduce the tensor-valued population loss function

F^{t}\colon[0,1]^{n\times n\times L}\times[0,1]^{n\times n\times L}\to\mathbb{R}^{n\times n\times L}

which maps a candidate pair
(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime})
to a tensor whose
(i,j,l)
-th entry is

\displaystyle F^{t}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{\prime}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime}_{i,j,l})=

\displaystyle-\big[\big\{\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\log(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{\prime}_{i,j,l})+(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t})\log(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{\prime}_{i,j,l})\big\}(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\big\{\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\log(\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime}_{i,j,l})+(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{t})\log(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\prime}_{i,j,l})\big\}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}\big],

where for any
t\in[T]
,
\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{t}=\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}
.
For any
t\in[T]
and any window length
k\in[t]
, we define the averaged population losses as

F^{t,k}=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{u=t-k+1}^{t}F^{u}.

Proof of Theorem
18
.

For any
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
, define

\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{t}=\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}^{t}\}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{t,k}=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}.

Note that for any
t\in[T]
,
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
, since
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\in[c_{\min},1-c_{\min}]
, we can derive the following bounds

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathbf{A}^{t}_{i,j,l}=1\mid\mathbf{A}^{t-1}_{i,j,l}=1\big\}\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathbf{A}^{t-1}_{i,j,l}=1\big\}+\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathbf{A}^{t}_{i,j,l}=1\mid\mathbf{A}^{t-1}_{i,j,l}=0\big\}\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathbf{A}^{t-1}_{i,j,l}=0\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}+\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}\big)\geq c_{\min}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}+c_{\min}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}\big)=c_{\min}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t}=

\displaystyle\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}+\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-c_{\min})\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}+(1-c_{\min})\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}\big)\leq 1-c_{\min}.

Thus, by
Definition

2
, we can conclude that

c_{\min}\leq\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t}\leq 1-c_{\min},\quad\forall t\in[T]\cup\{0\}.

(133)

Step 1.

In this step, we will show that with high probability for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
,
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
,

f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ and }F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ are }\big(\log(2),\psi(k)\big)\mbox{-close}.

(134)

where
\psi(k)=C_{\psi}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)/k
with a constant
C_{\psi}>0
.

Step 1.1.
In this sub-step, we will derive a concentration inequality for
\nabla f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
around
\nabla F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
for any
t\in[T]
,
k\in[t]
and
(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
, where
\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}=\{(\theta,\delta)^{\top}\in[c_{\min},1-c_{\min}]^{2}\}
.

By
Lemma

13
, for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
, the sequence
\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is strongly mixing.
Since the mixing property is hereditary, any process defined as a measurable function of finitely many
\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t}
’s is also strongly mixing. In particular, the processes

\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t-1})\big\}_{t\geq 1},\quad\big\{(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t})\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t-1}\big\}_{t\geq 1}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\big\{(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t})(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t-1})\big\}_{t\geq 1}

are also strongly mixing.
For any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
,
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
, let

\mathbf{Y}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\Big|k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\Big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})-\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})\big\}\Big\}\Big|,

\widetilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\Big|k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\Big\{(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u})(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})-\mathbb{E}\big\{(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u})(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})\big\}\Big\}\Big|,

\mathbf{X}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\Big|k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\Big\{(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u})\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}-\mathbb{E}\big\{(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u})\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}\big\}\Big\}\Big|,

and

\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\Big|k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\Big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}-\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}\big\}\Big\}\Big|.

By applying the Bernstein inequality for
\alpha
-mixing sequences
(
Merlevède et al. 2009
, e.g. Theorem 1 in)
, we obtain that

\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathbf{Y}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\geq C_{0}x\big\}\leq\exp\bigg\{-\frac{c_{0}kx^{2}}{1+x\log(k)\log\log(k)}\bigg\}.

where
C_{0}>0
is sufficiently large constant and
c_{0}>10
is an absolute constant.
Setting

x=\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k}},\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(k)\log\log(k)}{k}\bigg\},

yields

\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathbf{Y}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\geq C_{0}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k}},\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(k)\log\log(k)}{k}\bigg\}\leq(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{0}/2}.

Similarly, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\max\Big\{\mathbf{Y}_{i,j,l}^{t,k},\widetilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k},\mathbf{X}_{i,j,l}^{t,k},\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\Big\}\leq C_{0}\max\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k}},

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(k)\log\log(k)}{k}\bigg)\bigg\}\geq 1-4(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{0}/2}.

(135)

For any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
,
t\in[T]
,
k\in[t]
and
\theta,\delta\in[c,1-c]
, define

\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\theta)=-k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\bigg\{\frac{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})}{\theta}-\frac{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})}{1-\theta}\bigg\},

\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\delta)=-k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\bigg\{\frac{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}{\delta}-\frac{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}{1-\delta}\bigg\},

\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\theta)=-k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\bigg\{\frac{\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})\}}{\theta}-\frac{\mathbb{E}\{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})\}}{1-\theta}\bigg\},

\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\delta)=-k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\bigg\{\frac{\mathbb{E}\{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\}}{\delta}-\frac{\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\}}{1-\delta}\bigg\}.

Then we have

\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}}f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})=\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big),\quad\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}}f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})=\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big),

\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}}F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})=\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big),\quad\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}}F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})=\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\nabla f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})-\nabla F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\|_{2}^{2}=

\displaystyle\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}.

Then

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})-\nabla F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big[\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}\Big].

(136)

Now we focus on the term

\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big[\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}\Big].

Let
\mathcal{N}
be an
\varepsilon
-net of
\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
, then we have that

|\mathcal{N}|\leq(1+2\varepsilon^{-1})^{2},

(137)

and for every
\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
, there exists
\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}),\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l})\big)^{\top}\in\mathcal{N}
such that

\big\|\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)^{\top}-\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta})_{i,j,l},,\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)^{\top}\big\|_{2}\leq\varepsilon.

Since

\displaystyle\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\Big\{\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2},

it follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{N}}\Big[\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big[\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big[\Big\{\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\pi(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I.1)+(I.2)+(I.3).

(138)

For term
(I.1)
, by the fact that
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle(I.1)\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big\{2\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{-2}\big(\mathbf{Y}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}+2(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})^{-2}\big(\widetilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}+2\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{-2}\big(\mathbf{X}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+2(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{-2}\big(\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big[2c_{\min}^{-2}\big\{\big(\mathbf{Y}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}+\big(\widetilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}+\big(\mathbf{X}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}+\big(\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}\big\}\Big].

By (
D.2.1
), (
137
) and a union bound argument, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{(I.1)\geq 8C_{0}^{2}c_{\min}^{-2}\max\bigg(\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k},\frac{\log^{2}(n\vee L\vee T)\log^{2}(k)\{\log\log(k)\}^{2}}{k^{2}}\bigg)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(1+2\epsilon^{-1})^{2}(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{0}/2}.

(139)

For term
(I.2)
, we have that

\displaystyle(I.2)=

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\bigg[\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})\cdot\bigg\{\frac{1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})(1-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\frac{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}}{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})}\bigg\}\bigg]^{2}+\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\bigg[\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l})\big)k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\bigg\{\frac{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})(1-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}))}+\frac{1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}}{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l})}\bigg\}\bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{\min}^{-4}\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\big\{\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)^{2}+(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}))^{2}\big\}\leq c_{\min}^{-4}\epsilon^{2}.

(140)

For term
(I.3)
, we have that

\displaystyle(I.3)=

\displaystyle\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\bigg[\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\cdot\bigg\{\frac{\mathbb{E}\big\{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big\}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})(1-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\frac{\mathbb{E}\big\{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big\}}{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})}\bigg\}\bigg]^{2}+\bigg[\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l})\big)k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\bigg\{\frac{\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big\}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})(1-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}))}-\frac{\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})}{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l})}\bigg\}\bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{\min}^{-4}\bigg[\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\cdot\Big\{(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l})(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{u}(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})\Big\}\bigg]^{2}+c^{-4}\bigg[\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l})\big)k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\Big\{(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{u})\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{u}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}\Big\}\bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{\min}^{-4}\big\{\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}-\pi(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})\big)^{2}+(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}-\pi({\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}))^{2}\big\}\leq c_{\min}^{-4}\epsilon^{2}.

(141)

where the second inequality follows from (
133
).
Combining (
D.2.1
), (
D.2.1
), (
D.2.1
) and (
D.2.1
), we can derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\geq 8C_{0}^{2}c_{\min}^{-2}\max\bigg(\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k},\frac{\log^{2}(n\vee L\vee T)\log^{2}(k)\{\log\log(k)\}^{2}}{k^{2}}\bigg)+2c_{\min}^{-4}\varepsilon^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(1+2\epsilon^{-1})^{2}(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{0}/2}.

Choosing
\varepsilon=k^{-1/2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{\top}\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\Big\{\tilde{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\tilde{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\Big\{\bar{f}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)-\bar{F}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big\}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\geq C_{1}\max\bigg(\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k},\frac{\log^{2}(n\vee L\vee T)\log^{2}(k)\log\log^{2}(k)}{k^{2}}\bigg)\bigg\}\leq 36(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{0}/2},

where
C_{1}=8C_{0}c^{-2}+2c_{\min}^{-4}
.
Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}=

\displaystyle\bigg\{\sup_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}\|\nabla f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})-\nabla F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{C_{1}}\max\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)}{k}},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(k)\log\log(k)}{k}\bigg),\quad\forall 1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L],t\in[T],k\in[t]\bigg\},

(142)

By (
D.2.1
) and a union bound argument, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B})\geq 1-36\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{t}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{j}\sum_{l=1}^{L}(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{0}/2}=1-(n\vee L\vee T)^{-c_{1}},

(143)

where
c_{1}>0
is a constant. In the following steps, we assume the event
\mathcal{B}
holds.

Step 1.2.
In this sub-step, we will prove (
134
) under the the event
\mathcal{B}
defined in (
D.2.1
).

For any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
,
t\in[T]
,
k\in[t]
and
(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
, let the Hessian matrix of
F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})
be denoted by
H^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathbb{R}^{2\times 2}
.
It is straightforward to verify that
H^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})
is diagonal matrix, and thus its minimum eigenvalue is given by

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(H^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})\}}{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{2}}+\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\mathbb{E}\{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})(1-\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l})\}}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}\big)^{2}},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\mathbb{E}\{(1-\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l})\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\}}{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{2}}+\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}^{u}_{i,j,l}\mathbf{A}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})}{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{2}}+\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l})(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1})}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l})^{2}},

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{u}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}}{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{2}}+\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{u})\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2c_{\min}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}\min\bigg\{k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}),k^{-1}\sum_{u=t-(k-1)}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{u-1}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2},

(144)

where the first inequality follows from for any
t\in[T]
,
(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
and the last inequality follows from (
133
). Similarly, the largest eigenvalue of
H^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})
is

\displaystyle\lambda_{\max}(H^{t,k}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}))\leq 2c_{\min}^{-2}(1-c_{\min})^{2}.

Thus,
F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}
is
2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}
-strongly convex and
2c_{\min}^{-2}(1-c_{\min})^{2}
-smooth on
\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
.
By Part 3 of Lemma 5.1 in
Huang and Wang 2024
and the event
\mathcal{B}
defined in (
D.2.1
), it holds that
under the event
\mathcal{B}
, for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
,
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
,

f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ and }F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ are }\big(\log(2),\psi(k)\big)\mbox{-close},

where
\psi(k)=C_{\psi}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)/k
, with a constant
C_{\psi}>0
.

Step 2.
In this step, we will show that for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
and
t,t^{\prime}\in[T]
,
F^{t}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}
are

\Big(\log\{4(1-c_{\min})^{4}/c_{\min}^{4}\},c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}\big(|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}|^{2}+|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}|^{2}\big)\Big)\text{-close}.

By
Step 1.2
, for any
t\in[T]
,
F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}
is
2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}
-strongly convex and
2(1-c_{\min})^{2}c_{\min}^{-2}
-smooth. Since for any

,
F^{t}_{i,j,l}=F^{t,1}_{i,j,l}
, it follows that
F^{t}_{i,j,l}
is
2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}
-strongly convex and
2c_{\min}^{-2}(1-c_{\min})^{2}
-smooth on
\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
.
By Part 4 in Lemma 5.1 in
Huang and Wang 2024
, for any
t,t^{\prime}\in[T]
,
F_{t}
and
F_{t^{\prime}}
are

\Big(\log\{4(1-c_{\min})^{4}/c_{\min}^{4}\},c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}\big(|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}|^{2}+|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}|^{2}\big)\Big)\text{-close},

in
\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}
.

Step 3.
In this step, we show that under the event
\mathcal{B}
, for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
and
t\in[T_{g}]/[T_{g-1}]
with
g\in[G]
,

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\leq C_{2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\},

for some constant
C_{2}>0
.

By
Step 1
, we have that for any
k\in[t]
, under the event
\mathcal{B}
,

f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ and }F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ are }\big(\log(2),\psi(k)\big)\mbox{-close},

(145)

where
\psi(k)=C_{\psi}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)/k
with a constant
C_{\psi}>0
. Let
\bar{k}_{t}=\max\{k\in\mathcal{G}^{(t)}\colon k\leq t-{T_{g-1}}\}
.
By
Step 2
and
Definition

4
, we have that

F^{t-(\bar{k}_{t}-1)}_{i,j,l},\ldots,F^{t}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ are }\Big(\log\{4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\},\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\Big)\mbox{-close to }F_{t}

where
\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})=C_{\phi}\big(V(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big)^{2}
with

. Then by Part 7 of Lemma 5.2 in
Huang and Wang 2024
, for any
u\in[\bar{k}_{t}]

F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ and }F^{t}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ are }\Big(\log\{4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\},C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\Big)\mbox{-close},

where
C_{\phi^{\prime}}=4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}+1
.

Let
\hat{k}_{t}
be defined in
Algorithm

2
with

\tau(u)=C_{\tau}\max\Big\{u^{-1}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T),\big(V(u)\big)^{2}\Big\},\quad\forall u\in[t],

where
C_{\tau}>0
is a sufficiently large constant.

Step 3.1
In this sub-step, we will first prove that
\hat{k}_{t}\geq\bar{k}_{t}
. To this end, it suffices to show that

\max_{1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L]}\big\{f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big\}\leq\tau(u),\forall u\in\{k\in\mathcal{G}^{(t)}:k\leq\bar{k}_{t}\}.

For any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
, we have that

f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})=0.

Since
f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log(2),\psi(\bar{k}_{t}))
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big\{f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+\psi(\bar{k}_{t})\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\psi(\bar{k}_{t}).

Since
F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t}_{i,j,l}
are
\big(\log\{4(1-c_{\min})^{4}c_{\min}^{-4}\},C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1}\big)
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\big\{F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\big\{2\psi(\bar{k}_{t})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big\}.

For all
u\in\{k\in\mathcal{G}^{(t)}:k\leq\bar{k}_{t}\}
, since
F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log\{4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\},C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\big\{F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}}F^{t}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\Big[4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\big\{2\psi(\bar{k}_{t})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big\}+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{3}\psi(\bar{k}_{t})+C_{4}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})

where
C_{3}=32c_{\min}^{-8}(1-c_{\min})^{8}
and
C_{4}=4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\{4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}+1\}C_{\phi^{\prime}}
.
Since
f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log(2),\psi(u))
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big\{F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+\psi(u)\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2C_{3}\psi(\bar{k}_{t})+2C_{4}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})+\psi(u)\leq C_{5}\psi(u)+2C_{4}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1}),

where
C_{5}=2C_{3}+1
.
Since
C_{\tau}
is a sufficiently large constant and
V
is a non-increasing function, the last inequality above implies for all
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
and
u\in\{k\in\mathcal{G}^{(t)}:k\leq\bar{k}_{t}\}
,

f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\leq\tau(u),

which leads to

\max_{1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L]}\big\{f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta})_{i,j,l}\big\}\leq\tau(u).

This shows that
\hat{k}_{t}\geq\bar{k}_{t}
.

Step 3.2.
Since
\hat{k}_{t}\geq\bar{k}_{t}
, by the definition of
\hat{k}_{t}
,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L]}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\leq\tau(\bar{k}_{t}).

Since for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
,
f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log(2),\psi(\bar{k}_{t}))
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big[f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+\psi(\bar{k}_{t})\big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big[\tau(\bar{k}_{t})+\psi(\bar{k}_{t})\big]\leq 4\tau(\bar{k}_{t}).

Since
F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log\{4(1-c_{\min})^{4}/c_{\min}^{4}\},C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\big\{F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\{4\tau(\bar{k}_{t})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\}\leq 20c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\tau(\bar{k}_{t}).

Since
\bar{k}_{t}\geq\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}
, we have that

\displaystyle F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20C_{\tau}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{4}}{c_{\min}^{4}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}},\big[V\big(\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}\big)\big]^{2}\bigg\},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20C_{\tau}^{\prime}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{4}}{c_{\min}^{4}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\},

where
C_{\tau}^{\prime}>0
is an absolute constant and the last inequality follows from
2
(i)
. Then, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20C_{\tau}^{\prime}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{4}}{c_{\min}^{4}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}.

Note that since
F^{t}_{i,j,l}
is
2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}
-strongly convex, then

\displaystyle F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}}F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)+\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}}F^{t}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{2c_{\min}^{2}}{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}\Big\{\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2c_{\min}^{2}}{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}\Big\{\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\Big\}.

Thus, we finally have that under the event
\mathcal{B}
,

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{10C_{\tau}^{\prime}(1-c_{\min})^{6}}{c_{\min}^{6}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\},

which completes the proof.

∎

D.2.2
Proof of Proposition
19

Proof of
Proposition

19
.

Without loss of generality, assume
t\in[T_{g}]\backslash[T_{g-1}]
. Define

\bar{k}_{t}=\max\{k\in\mathcal{G}^{(t)}\colon k\leq t-{T_{g-1}}\}.

(146)

By
Step 3.1
in the proof of
Theorem

18
, for any
t\in[T]
, under the event
\mathcal{B}
defined in (
D.2.1
), we have
\hat{k}_{t}\geq\bar{k}_{t}
.

We now claim that under the event
\mathcal{B}
, for any
s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-\hat{k}_{t}]
,

\displaystyle|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\leq C^{\prime}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}}},V(t-T_{g-1})\bigg\}

(147)

and

\displaystyle|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\leq C^{\prime}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}}},V(t-T_{g-1})\bigg\},

(148)

where
C^{\prime}>0
is an absolute constant.
From now on, we assume the event
\mathcal{B}
holds.

This proof consists of four steps. In
Steps 1-3
, we will prove the claim above. In
Step 4
, by applying
Proposition

15
and the claim above, we will conclude the proof.

Step 1.

Let
\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}=[c_{\min},1-c_{\min}]^{2}
. By
Algorithm

2
, we can derive that

\max_{1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L]}\big\{f^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big\}\leq\tau(\hat{k}_{t}),

By
Step 1
in the proof of
Theorem

18
, for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
,
l\in[L]
and
k\in[t]
, we have that

f^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ and }F^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\mbox{ are }\big(\log(2),\psi(k)\big)\mbox{-close},

(149)

where
\psi(k)=C_{\psi}\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)/k
with a constant
C_{\psi}>0
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\leq\tau(\hat{k}_{t})+\psi(\hat{k}_{t}).

(150)

Let

\big(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)=\arginf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}).

Since
F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
is
2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}
-strongly convex, we obtain that

\displaystyle F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\widetilde{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)\cdot\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}}F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)+\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\cdot\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}}F^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)+\frac{2c_{\min}^{2}}{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}\Big\{\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2c_{\min}^{2}}{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}\Big\{\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\Big\}.

(151)

Combining (
150
) and (
D.2.2
), we have that

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}{2c_{\min}^{2}}\big\{\tau(\hat{k}_{t})+\psi(\hat{k}_{t})\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{\hat{k}_{t}},\big\{V(\hat{k}_{t})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{\bar{k}_{t}},\big\{V(\bar{k}_{t})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}},\big[V\big(\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}\big)\big]^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}

(152)

where
C_{1},C_{2}>0
are absolute constants, the third inequality follows from
\hat{k}_{t}\geq\bar{k}_{t}
and
V
is non-increasing, the forth inequality follows from
\bar{k}_{t}\geq\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}
and
V
is non-increasing, and the final inequality follows from
2
(i)
.

Step 2.
Since
\hat{k}_{t}\geq\bar{k}_{t}
, by the definition of
\hat{k}_{t}
in
Algorithm

2
,

\max_{1\leq i\leq j\leq n,l\in[L]}\big\{f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\big\}\leq\tau(\bar{k}_{t}).

Since for any
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
,
f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log(2),\psi(\bar{k}_{t}))
-close, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\big\{f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}f^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+\psi(\bar{k}_{t})\big\}\leq 2\big\{\tau(\bar{k}_{t})+\psi(\bar{k}_{t})\big\}\leq 4\tau(\bar{k}_{t}).

Since for any
u\in[t]\backslash[t-T_{g}]
,
F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}
and
F^{u}_{i,j,l}
are
(\log\{4(1-c_{\min})^{4}/c_{\min}^{4}\},C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})
-close, we can derive that

\displaystyle F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\big\{F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{t,\bar{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\{4\tau(\bar{k}_{t})+C_{\phi^{\prime}}\phi(T_{g}-T_{g-1})\}\leq 20c_{\min}^{-4}(1-c_{\min})^{4}\tau(\bar{k}_{t}),

where the last inequality follows from
\bar{k}_{t}\leq t-T_{g-1}\leq T_{g}-T_{g-1}
and
V
is non-increasing.
Since
\bar{k}_{t}\geq\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}
and
V
is non-increasing, for any
u\in[t]\backslash[t-T_{g}]
, we have that

\displaystyle F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-\inf_{(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})\in\mathcal{Q}^{\prime}}F^{u}_{i,j,l}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20C_{\tau}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{4}}{c_{\min}^{4}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}},\big[V\big(\max\{t-2^{\lceil\log_{2}(T_{g-1})\rceil},1\}\big)\big]^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20C_{\tau}^{\prime}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{4}}{c_{\min}^{4}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}

where
C_{\tau}^{\prime}>0
is an absolute constant and the last inequality follows from
2
(i)
. Thus, for any
u\in[t]\backslash[t-T_{g}]
, it holds that

\displaystyle F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20C_{\tau}^{\prime}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{4}}{c_{\min}^{4}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}.

Since
F^{u}_{i,j,l}
is
2c_{\min}^{2}(1-c_{\min})^{-2}
-strongly convex, then

\displaystyle F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big)-F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}}F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)+\big(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)\nabla_{\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}}F^{u}_{i,j,l}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{2c_{\min}^{2}}{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}\Big\{\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2c_{\min}^{2}}{(1-c_{\min})^{2}}\Big\{\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\Big\}.

Therefore, for any
u\in[t]\backslash[t-T_{g}]
, we have that

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 10C_{\tau}^{\prime}\frac{(1-c_{\min})^{6}}{c_{\min}^{6}}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}.

(153)

Step 3.

By (
D.2.2
) and (
D.2.2
), under the event
\mathcal{B}
, for any
u\in[t]\backslash[t-T_{g}]
, we have that

\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\leq C_{3}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\},

and

\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}+\big|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\leq C_{3}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\},

where
C_{3}>0
is an absolute constant.
By
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
, for any
u\in[t]\backslash[t-T_{g}]
, we obtain that

\big|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\leq 2C_{3}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\},

(154)

and

\big|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big|^{2}\leq 2C_{3}\max\bigg\{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}},\big\{V(t-T_{g-1})\big\}^{2}\bigg\}.

Note that we can derive that for any
u\in[t]\backslash[T_{g-1}]
,

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|=

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}}{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\ \big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)}{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)}\bigg|.

Choose

. Then we obtain that

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|=

\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\frac{\sum_{s=(T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1)\vee(t-\hat{k}_{t}+1)}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}+1}\wedge t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)}{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)}\Bigg|.

(155)

Choosing

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{t,\hat{k}_{t}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g-1}+1}_{i,j,l}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\bigg\}^{-1}\bigg|\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\sum_{s=(T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1)\vee(t-\hat{k}_{t}+1)}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}+1}\wedge t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{{}_{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g-1}+1}_{i,j,l}+\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}\big)\bigg|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\Big|\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\sum_{s=(T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1)\vee(t-\hat{k}_{t}+1)}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}+1}\wedge t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{{}_{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g-1}+1}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big|}{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\frac{\Big|\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\sum_{s=(T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1)\vee(t-\hat{k}_{t}+1)}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}+1}\wedge t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big)\Big|}{\sum_{s=t-\hat{k}_{t}+1}^{t}\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I)-(II)

(156)

, for any

\displaystyle(IV)\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\big(p_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}+n_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{p}_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{n}_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}\big)+\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}}^{g-1}\Big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}}_{i,j,l}\big|\Big)

\displaystyle\frac{1+C_{\alpha}}{1+C_{\alpha}}\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\big(p_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}-n_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{p}_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{n}_{i,j,l}^{g^{\prime\prime}}\big)+\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}}^{g-1}\Big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}}_{i,j,l}\big|\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+C_{\alpha}}{1+C_{\alpha}}\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}-1}^{g-1}\Big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}+1}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}+1}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}\big|\Big)+\sum_{g^{\prime\prime}=g^{\prime}}^{g-1}\Big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}+1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{T_{g^{\prime\prime}}}_{i,j,l}\big|

\displaystyle C^{\prime\prime}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee L\vee T)\log(T)\log\log(T)}{t-T_{g-1}}},V(t-T_{g-1})\bigg\}
