D.3
Proofs for Appendix
D.1

This section provides the proofs of the results stated in Appendix
D.1
. The proofs are organized as follows: Lemma
13
is proved in Appendix
D.3.1
, Lemma
14
in Appendix
D.3.2
, Proposition
15
in Appendix
D.3.3
and Lemma
16
in Appendix
D.3.4
.

D.3.1
Proof of Lemma
13

Proof of
Lemma

13
.

Fix
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
. For any
a,b\in\mathbb{N}
with
a\leq b
, define the event

\mathcal{R}^{a,b}_{i,j,l}=\bigcap_{t=a}^{b}\{\mathbf{E}_{i,j,l}^{t}=0\}.

Since
\{E_{i,j,l}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
are independent, we have that

\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{R}^{a,b}_{i,j,l})=\prod_{t=a}^{b}(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{t}).

For any
t,k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
, let
\mathcal{F}^{0,t}_{i,j,l}=\sigma(\mathbf{A}^{0}_{i,j,l},\mathbf{A}^{1}_{i,j,l},\cdots,\mathbf{A}^{t}_{i,j,l})
and
\mathcal{F}^{t+k,\infty}_{i,j,l}=\sigma(\mathbf{A}^{t+k}_{i,j,l},\mathbf{A}^{t+k+1}_{i,j,l},\cdots)
. For any
\mathcal{A}\in\mathcal{F}^{0,t}_{i,j,l}
and
\mathcal{B}\in\mathcal{F}^{t+k,\infty}_{i,j,l}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})+\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})+\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\mid(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big)\mathbb{P}\big((\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})+\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{A}\mid(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big)\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{B}\mid(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big)\mathbb{P}\big((\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})+\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{A})\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{B}\cap(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}\big),

where the third equality follows from the conditional independence of
\mathcal{A}
and
\mathcal{B}
given
(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})^{c}
, and the last equality follows from the independence between
\mathcal{A}
and
\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l}
. Thus, we can derive that

\displaystyle|\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B})-\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B})|

\displaystyle=|\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})-\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})=\prod_{j=t+1}^{t+k}(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i,j,l}^{t})\leq(1-2c_{\min})^{k},

where the first inequality follows from the fact that
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})
and
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B}\cap\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})
both lie in
\big[0,\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{R}^{t+1,t+k}_{i,j,l})\big]
.

Therefore, for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
,

\alpha(k)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}\sup_{\mathcal{A}\in\mathcal{F}^{0,t}_{i,j,l},\mathcal{B}\in\mathcal{F}^{t+k,\infty}_{i,j,l}}|\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}\cap\mathcal{B})-\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B})|\leq(1-2c_{\min})^{k}.

Since
\alpha(k)\to 0
as
k\to\infty
, by
Definition

6
, the process
\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is strongly mixing, which completes the proof.

∎

D.3.2
Proof of Lemma
14

Proof of
Lemma

14
.

Fix
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
. Without loss of generality, assume
1\leq r\leq s
.
For
s>r
, define the indicator of no update between
r
and
s

N^{r,s}=\prod_{t=r+1}^{s}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathbf{E}^{t}_{i,j,l}=0\}.

Note that

\mathbb{E}\{N^{r,s}\}=\prod_{t=r+1}^{s}(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l}).

(168)

Next, define the first update time

\tau=\min\big\{t\in[s]\backslash[r]\colon\mathbf{E}_{i,j,l}^{t}\in\{\pm 1\}\big\},

with the convention that
\tau=\infty
if the set is empty. Then define

\widetilde{B}=\begin{cases}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathbf{E}_{i,j,l}^{\tau}=1\},&\tau<\infty,\\
0,&\tau=\infty.\end{cases}

By recursively applying
Definition

2
, we obtain that

\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}=N^{r,s}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}+(1-N^{r,s})\widetilde{B}.

Since
N^{r,s}
and
\widetilde{B}
depend only on
\{\mathbf{E}_{i,j,l}^{t}\}_{t=r+1}^{s}
and are thus independent of
\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}
, we
compute the covariance

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}\big(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r},\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}\big)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}\big\}-\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\}\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big\{N^{r,s}(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r})^{2}+(1-N^{r,s})\widetilde{B}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\big\}-\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\}\mathbb{E}\big\{N^{r,s}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}+(1-N^{r,s})\widetilde{B}\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{N^{r,s}\}\mathbb{E}\big\{(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r})^{2}\big\}+\mathbb{E}\big\{(1-N^{r,s})\widetilde{B}\big\}\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\big\}-\big[\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\}\big]^{2}\mathbb{E}\{N^{r,s}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\mathbb{E}\big\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}\big\}\mathbb{E}\big\{(1-N^{r,s})\widetilde{B}\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\{N^{r,s}\}\mathrm{Var}(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}),

(169)

Since
\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r}
is a Bernoulli random variable, we have that

\mathrm{Var}(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r})\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(170)

If
r=s
, the bound is immediate

0\leq\mathrm{Cov}(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r},\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s})\leq\frac{1}{4}=\frac{1}{4}(1-2c_{\min})^{s-r};

and if
r<s
, combining (
168
), (
D.3.2
) and (
170
), we have that

0\leq\mathrm{Cov}(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r},\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s})\leq\frac{1}{4}\prod_{t=r+1}^{s}(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{t}_{i,j,l})\leq\frac{1}{4}(1-2c_{\min})^{s-r}.

These conclude the proof.
∎

D.3.3
Proof of Proposition
15

Proof of
Proposition

15
.

Fix
(i,j,l)
with
1\leq i\leq j\leq n
and
l\in[L]
, and consider a look-back window of length
k
ending at time
t
, i.e. observations
\{\mathbf{A}^{s}\}_{s=t-k+1}^{t}
. Recall that the estimators are defined as

\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})}{1-k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}},\quad\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Delta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s})\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}}{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}}.

For any
t\in[T]
and
s\in[t]\cup\{0\}
, write

\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{s}=\mathbb{E}\{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}\}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}=\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}.

Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{t,k}=\bigg\{\bigg|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\bigg|\leq\frac{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}{2}\bigg\}.

(171)

Expanding
(1-k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})^{-1}
around
(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{-1}
yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})}{1-k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\bigg\{\frac{1}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}+\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{u=2}^{\infty}\frac{\big(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u}}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u+1}}\bigg\}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}\bigg\}+\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\sum_{u=2}^{\infty}\frac{\big(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u}}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u+1}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}\bigg\}+\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\sum_{u=2}^{\infty}\frac{\big(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u}}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u+1}}\bigg\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\sum_{u=2}^{\infty}\frac{\big(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u}}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u+1}}\bigg\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}^{c}}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I)+(II)+(III.1)+(III.2).

(172)

In the following steps, we address the above terms separately.

Step 1.

In this step, we analyze term
(I)
in (
D.3.3
). First, note that

\displaystyle(I)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{1}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}\sum_{s,u=t-k+1}^{t}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l})(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{1}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}+\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}

(173)

If
k=1
, then we can directly have that

\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}=0
.
For any
v\in[t]\backslash[t-k]
, we have that

\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}=\frac{1}{k^{2}}\sum_{s,u=t-k+1}^{t}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l})(\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{v-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{1}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}.

(174)

For any
\pi,\theta,\delta\in[c_{\min},1-c_{\min}]
, define

F_{\pi}(\theta,\delta)=\theta+(1-\theta-\delta)\pi.

Then for any
\pi\in[c_{\min},1-c_{\min}]
, we have that

\frac{\partial F_{\pi}}{\partial\theta}=1-\pi,\quad\mbox{and}\quad\frac{\partial F_{\pi}}{\partial\delta}=-\pi.

Consequently, it holds that

\|\nabla F_{\pi}\|_{\infty}\leq 1-c_{\min}\leq C_{\pi},

where the last inequality follows from
C_{\pi}\geq 1
.
Then we have

\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u}_{i,j,l}=F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)\quad\mbox{and}\quad\boldsymbol{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}=F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-2}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big).

We claim that under
2
(iii)
, for any
u\geq T_{g}+2
,

\bigl|\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\leq C_{\pi}\sum_{v=T_{g}+2}^{u}(1-2c_{\min})^{u-v}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big).

(175)

When
u=T_{g}+2
, inequality (
175
) follows directly from
2
(iii)
.
Assume now that for some
u\geq T_{g}+3
,

\bigl|\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-2}_{i,j,l}\big|\leq C_{\pi}\sum_{v=T_{g}+2}^{u-1}(1-2c_{\min})^{u-v-1}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big).

We then have that

\displaystyle\big|\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big|F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)-F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-2}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big|F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}\big)-F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|+\big|F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big)-F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-2}_{i,j,l}}\big(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla F_{\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}}\big\|_{\infty}\Big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\Big)+\Big|\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big)\big(\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{u-2}_{i,j,l}\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\pi}\big\{\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big\}+C_{\pi}\sum_{v=T_{g}+2}^{u-1}(1-2c_{\min})^{u-v}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{\pi}\sum_{v=T_{g}+2}^{u}(1-2c_{\min})^{u-v}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big),

which proves the claim stated in (
175
).

Then for any
v\neq s
and
v,s\geq T_{g}+2
, we have that

\displaystyle\bigl|\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{v-1}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{s-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{v^{\prime}=v\wedge s}^{v\vee s-1}\bigl|\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{v^{\prime}=v\wedge s}^{v\vee s-1}C_{\pi}\sum_{u^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{v^{\prime}}(1-2c_{\min})^{v^{\prime}-u^{\prime}}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{u^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\pi}}{2c_{\min}}\sum_{v^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{v\vee s-1}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big),

(176)

where the last inequality follows from summing the geometric series
\sum_{a=0}^{\infty}(1-2c_{\min})^{a}\leq 1/(2c_{\min})
for
0<c_{\min}<1/2
.

Combining (
174
) and (
D.3.3
), we can derive that if
t-k\geq T_{g}+1
for some
g\in[G]
, then

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}\big|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}\frac{C_{\pi}}{2c_{\min}}\max_{s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-k]}|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\sum_{v^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{t-1}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\pi}}{2c_{\min}^{2}}\max_{s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-k]}|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\sum_{v^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{t-1}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big).

(177)

where the last inequality follows from (
133
).

Combining (
D.3.3
) and (
D.3.3
), we can conclude that

\displaystyle(I)=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}+\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l},

(178)

where if
k=1
,

\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}=0,

(179)

and if
t-k\geq T_{g}+1
for some
g\in[G]
, then

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}|\leq\frac{C_{\pi}}{2c_{\min}^{2}}\max_{s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-k]}|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\sum_{v^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{t-1}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big).

(180)

Step 2.
In this step, we focus on term
(III.1)
in (
D.3.3
). By Taylor series with Lagrange remainder, there exist a random scalar
r_{i,j,l}^{t,k}
between
k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}
such that

\displaystyle(III.1)=\frac{1}{k}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{\big(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}}{\big(1-r_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{3}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}}\bigg]\geq 0.

(181)

On the other hand, due to the event
\mathcal{I}_{t,k}
defined in (
171
), we can derive that

\displaystyle\sum_{u=2}^{\infty}\frac{\big|k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big|^{u}}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{u+1}}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\bigg)^{2}\sum_{u=0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{3}2^{u}}

\displaystyle=\bigg(k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\bigg)^{2}\cdot\frac{2}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{3}}.

We obtain that

\displaystyle(III.1)\leq\frac{1}{k}\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\bigg)\cdot\frac{2}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{3}}.

(182)

Then we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\bigg)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k}\sum_{r,s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathrm{Cov}\big(\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{r-1},\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\big)\leq\frac{1}{k}\sum_{r,s=t-k+1}^{t}\frac{1}{4}(1-2c_{\min})^{|r-s|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4k}\sum_{h=-(k-1)}^{k-1}(k-|h|)(1-2c_{\min})^{|h|}=\frac{1}{4k}\bigg\{k+2\sum_{h=1}^{k-1}(k-h)(1-2c_{\min})^{h}\bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4k}\bigg[k+\frac{(1-2c_{\min})\{1-(k-1)(1-2c_{\min})^{k}+k(1-2c_{\min})^{k+1}\}}{(2c_{\min})^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\bigg\{1+\frac{2(1-2c_{\min})}{k(2c_{\min})^{2}}\bigg\},

(183)

where the first inequality follows from
Lemma

14
.
Combining (
181
), (
182
) and (
D.3.3
), we can derive that

\displaystyle 0\leq(III.1)\leq\frac{1}{4k}\bigg\{1+\frac{2(1-2c_{\min})}{k(2c_{\min})^{2}}\bigg\}\frac{2}{c_{\min}^{3}}\leq k^{-1}C_{0},

(184)

for some absolute constant
C_{0}>0
.

Step 3.
In this step, we consider term
(II)
in (
D.3.3
).
By Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle|(II)|=

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}}\mathrm{Cov}\bigg(\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}),\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}}\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{k}\sum\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\bigg)}\cdot\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\bigg)}.

By (
D.3.3
) in
Step 2
, we obtain that

\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\bigg)\leq\frac{C_{1}^{\prime}}{k},

for some absolute constant
C_{1}^{\prime}>0
.
Similarly, we obtain that

\mathrm{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{k}\sum\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\bigg)\leq\frac{C_{2}^{\prime}}{k}.

Hence, by (
133
), it holds that

|(II)|\leq\frac{1}{c_{\min}^{2}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}^{\prime}C_{2}^{\prime}}{k^{2}}}\leq k^{-1}C_{1},

(185)

for some absolute constant
C_{1}>0
.

Step 4.
Analogous to the expansion in (
D.3.3
), we obtain that

\displaystyle(III.2)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}-\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{2}}\bigg\}\mathbbm{1}_{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}^{c}}\bigg].

Note that

\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\in[0,1]
.
By (
133
), we have that

\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})}{1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}\in[0,c_{\min}^{-1}]

and

\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s}(1-\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1})\frac{k^{-1}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}}{(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k})^{2}}\in[-c_{\min}^{-2},c_{\min}^{-2}].

Thus,

|(III.2)|\leq\big({c_{\min}^{-2}}+c_{\min}^{-1}\big)\mathbb{P}\{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}^{c}\}.

If
1\leq k\leq 3
, since
\frac{1}{k}\sum_{s=t-k+1}^{t}\mathbf{A}_{i,j,l}^{s-1}\in[0,1]
, we have that

\mathbb{P}\{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}^{c}\}\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-\frac{\big(1-\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big)^{2}}{2}\bigg\}\leq 2\exp\big\{-c_{\min}^{2}/2\big\}\leq C_{2}k^{-1}

for some absolute constant
C_{2}>0
.

If
k\geq 4
, applying the Bernstein inequality for
\alpha
-mixing sequences
(
Merlevède et al. 2009
, e.g. Theorem 1 in)
together with (
133
) yields

\mathbb{P}\{\mathcal{I}_{t,k}^{c}\}\leq\exp\bigg\{-\frac{c_{0}k}{\log(k)\log\log(k)}\bigg\}\leq\frac{C_{3}}{k}

for some absolute constant
C_{3}>0
. Hence, it holds that

\displaystyle|(III.2)|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{4}}{k},

(187)

for some absolute constant
C_{4}>0

Step 5.
Combining (
D.3.3
), (

), (

), (
180
), (

), (
185
) and (

), we have that

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbb{E}\big\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\big\}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}\Big|\leq

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{i,j,l}^{t,k}|+\frac{C_{0}+C_{1}+C_{4}}{k},

where, if
k=1
,

\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}=0;

and if
t-k\geq T_{g}+1
for some
g\in[G]
, then

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{i,j,l}|\leq\frac{C_{\pi}}{2c_{\min}^{2}}\max_{s,u\in[t]\backslash[t-k]}|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{s}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{u}_{i,j,l}|\sum_{v^{\prime}=T_{g}+2}^{t-1}\big(\big|\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|+\big|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}}_{i,j,l}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{v^{\prime}-1}_{i,j,l}\big|\big).

This completes the proof of (
125
).
Similarly, we can prove (

D.3.4
Proof of Lemma
16

Proof of
Lemma

16
.

Each row of
Z
contains exactly one
1
and each column
k\in[K]
contains
s_{k}
ones, so

Z^{\top}Z=\mathrm{diag}(s_{1},\dots,s_{K}).

Therefore
\mathrm{rank}(Z)=K
,
\sigma_{1}(Z)=\sqrt{s_{\max}}
and

\sigma_{K}(Z)=\sqrt{s_{\min}}
.

For any
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
, it holds that

\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k}=\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}\times_{1}Z\times_{2}Z.

By the definition and basic properties of tensor matricisation
(
Zhang and Xia 2018
, e.g. Lemma 4 in )
, we see that

\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k})=Z\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})(Z\otimes I_{L})^{\top}.

Fix
t\in[T]
and
k\in[t]
. We aim to show that
\mathrm{rank}(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k}))=\mathrm{rank}(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}))
. By Lemma S3 in the Supplement of
Wang et al. 2025
, it suffices to verify that
Z\otimes I_{L}\in\mathbb{R}^{nL\times KL}
has full column rank, i.e.
\mathrm{rank}(Z\otimes I_{L})=KL
.
Using the property of Kronecker products, we obtain
\mathrm{rank}(Z\otimes I_{L})=\mathrm{rank}(Z)\mathrm{rank}(I_{L})=LK
. Thus
\mathcal{M}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{t,k})
and
\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})
have the same rank.

Next, we have that

\|\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k})\|\leq\|Z\|\|\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})\|\|Z\otimes I_{L}\|=\sigma_{1}^{2}(Z)\|\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})\|\leq s_{\max}\|\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}\|.

It follows from Lemma S4 in the Supplement of
Wang et al. 2025
that

\sigma_{\min}(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k}))\geq\sigma_{\min}(Z)\sigma_{\min}(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}))\sigma_{\min}(Z\otimes I_{L})=\sigma_{K}^{2}(Z)\sigma_{K}(\mathcal{M}_{1}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}))\geq s_{\min}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}).

Applying the same arguments, we obtain for any
s\in[3]
that

\mathrm{rank}\big(\mathcal{M}_{s}(\mathbf{\Omega}^{t,k}\big)=\mathrm{rank}(\mathcal{M}_{s}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}))

and

s_{\min}\sigma_{\min}(\mathbf{Q}^{t,k})\leq\sigma_{\min}\big(\mathcal{M}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{t,k})\big)\leq\big\|\mathcal{M}_{s}(\boldsymbol{\Omega}^{t,k})\big\|\leq s_{\max}\|\mathbf{Q}^{t,k}\|.

We complete the proof.

∎
